Revision 826
***New Features***
1. Add output of ice saturation and water saturation when water freezing/thawing is considered. Variable name is s_ice and s_water, respectively. 
2. Add support of 'extent of zone: distance constraint' for node/cell selection.
3. Add support of heat parameters update for ice when freezing/thawing is considered.
4. Add buffer for zone selection using polygon. The nodes/cells located within the buffer is considered as being selected.
5. Add zone selection using node/cell layer id. This feature is for layered mesh only. If nodes/cells are stored from top to bottom, then the top layer id is one and the bottom layer id is number of node/cell layers, and vice versa.

6. Add scaling factor for ice thickness.
7. Add scaling factor mineral reactivity.
8. Add 'extent of zone: top layer coordinates constraint' and 'extent of zone: bottom layer coordinates constraint'.
9. Add 'input units of mineral volume fractions' to use volume fraction of solid phase as mineral input unit. By default, the mineral input unit is volume fraction of bulk.
10. Add support of double precision output in ascii format. By default, the output in ascii format is single precision.
11. Add support of 'anticipated number of newton iterations' for flow and heat transport solver. The previous versions only have maximum number of newton iterations for flow and heat transport solver and use a constant to increase timestep. Sometimes this causes aggressive time step increasing which finally results into a lot of failed iterations.
12. Add 'ellipsoid-fitting model'. The thickness of ice sheet is described ellipsoid function (x-x0)^2/a^2 + (y-y0)^2/b^2 + (z-z0)^2/c^2 = 1 and rotation angle alpha along Z axis.
13. Add pre-exist lake/river pressure head to ice sheet thickness during ice sheet advance stage.
14. Add 'gradient-radius' type initial/boundary condition type. This is similar to 'gradient' type but use a radius value instead of x (or y, z) coordinate.
15. Add node and cell selection by distance range to specified point.
16. Add node and cell selection by distance range to specified line segment.
17. Add parallel spatial data input (e.g., read material properties and/or initial conditions from files) using master processor and then broadcast to all other processors. In the previous version, the data is read by all the processors. The performance depends on the system structure and networking bandwidth. There is no significant difference in performance between these two methods so far based on the tested case with 1 million nodes. 
18. Add feature to set memory limitation per processor so that when code crashes because of memory limitation, it reports error to the screen output and log file.

19. Add error information to screen output and log file when database file is not found or error in reading.
20. Add switch to turn off the respiration/exudation if the concentration is too low.
21. Add spatial and transient output of root density. The suffix is gsroot for spatial output and gbroot for transient output. 
22. Add option to include diffusive flux for aqueous phase for third type bounary condition. The previous version only has advective flux in the third type boundary condition.
23. Add 'limited output of transient data'.
24. Add output (prefix_o.rup) for total solute uptake, including passive uptake and respiration.
25. Add leaf recycling module. This module takes solute uptake to corresponding minerals and distribute them in the specified zone and time period. Two output file prefix_o.rup and prefix_o.rupcm are added. The file prefix_o.rup contains the solute uptake over time including passive uptake and respiration uptake. The file prefix_o.rupcm contains the recyclable solute uptake and returned mineral into specified zone over time. The latter includes total recyclable solute uptake and returned mineral in the current cycle as well as the past cycles. For both files, positive value means solute uptake from the porous media into the root while negative value means solute exudes from root into porous media.  
26. Add function to exclude diffusive flux from mixed boundary condition. 
27. Add anistropy and unstructured grid capability for multi-component diffusion.
28. Add user specified zone dependent loading factor. Loading factor can now be calculated based on mechanical parameters or user specified value.
29. Add spatial weighting for gradient average at control volume interface. This feature is only available for Green-Gauss and Least Square gradient reconstruction method. For high order least square method, no spatial weighting is required since the gradient is directly calculated at the control volume interface.

30. Port Fred's root related code to latest MIN3P version and fixed bugs. See usage and update.
31. Add legacy root uptake method, which is same as old version root water uptake formula.

32. Add root upscale factor for ArchiSimple code. These are linear factors in X, Y and Z directions. Please note these factors should be larger than zero.

33. Add keyword 'effective porosity/tortuosity factor for MCD diffusion'. The previous version uses a hard wired parameter 0.3 in the code. If not specified, a default value 0.3 will be used.
34. Assign default 'harmonic' spatial averaging for multicomponent diffusion if the spatial averaging for MCD is not specified. This is a required keyword to make MCD code work. The previous code does not check this and results are not correct if no spatial averaging is specified. 

35. Add first order decay for aqueous species. 
-->


***Usage***

1. No extra keyword is required. When water freezing/thawing is activated, this feature is  turned on automatically.

2. Add the keyword 'extent of zone: distance constraint' followed by reference point coordinations and distance range. Please note this keyword should be used together with general zone selection.

    'extent of zone: all nodes in box' 
    -1.0d10  1.0d10  0.0d0  0.0d0  -1.0d10  1.0d10

    'extent of zone: distance constraint'
    250.0  0.0  300.0  286.9  300.1 

3. Add the keyword 'update energy balance parameters for ice' into data block 'control parameters - energy balance' and add the heat capacity of ice and thermal conductivity of ice into data block 'physical parameters - energy balance'.

    !data block 'control parameters - energy balance'
    'update energy balance parameters for ice'

    !data block 'physical parameters - energy balance'
    'specific heat of ice'
    2108
   
    'ice thermal conductivity in x-direction'
    1.6d0

    'ice thermal conductivity in y-direction'
    1.6d0

    'ice thermal conductivity in z-direction'
    1.6d0

4. Add the keyword 'polygon zone selection buffer' followed by buffer value into data block 'spatial discretization'. By default, the buffer value is 1.0d-4. The unit is same as the input mesh unit.
    'polygon zone selection buffer'
    1.0d-6      ;default value is 1.0d-4 is the keyword is not specified. 

5. Add the keyword 'extent of zone: boundary nodes by layer id', or 'extent of zone: internal nodes by layer id' or 'extent of zone: all nodes by layer id' followed by layer id or layer id range. For cell selection, change 'nodes' to 'cells' An example is shown below. 
    'extent of zone: boundary nodes by layer id'
    'read data'
    2           ;number of layer id to read
    3           ;first layer id to read
    7           ;second layer id to read

    'extent of zone: boundary nodes by layer id'
    'read data range'
    2           ;number of layer id ranges to read
    3, 5        ;first layer id range to read
    7, 9        ;second layer id range to read


6. Add the keyword 'scaling factor of ice thickness' followed by scaling factor into data block 'control parameters - ice sheet loading/unloading'. An example is shown below.
    'scaling factor of ice thickness'
    2.0                 ;ice thickness will be multiplied by a factor of 2.0.

7. Add the keyword 'scaling factors of mineral reactivity' followed by scaling factor of each mineral reactivity into data block 'geochemical system'. An example is shown below.
    'scaling factors of mineral reactivity'
    1.0    ;mineral 1
    1.0    ;mineral 2
    5.0    ;mineral 3
    10.0   ;mineral 4
    0.1    ;mineral 5

8. Add the keyword 'extent of zone: top layer coordinates constraint' and/or 'extent of zone: bottom layer coordinates constraint' followed by elevation range. Please note this keyword should be used together with general zone selection.

    'extent of zone: all nodes in box' 
    -1.0d10  1.0d10  0.0d0  0.0d0  -1.0d10  1.0d10

    'extent of zone: top layer coordinates constraint'
    100.0  200.0        ;only the nodes whose top layer elevation between 100.0 and 200.0 are selected 
    
9. Add the keyword 'input units of mineral volume fractions' followed by unit into Data Block 7: control parameters - reactive transport. An example is shown below.
    'input units of mineral volume fractions'
    'volume fraction of solid phase'

10. Add keyword 'use double precision' into data block 'output control'.
    'output control'
    ...
    'use double precision'

11. Add the keyword 'anticipated number of newton iterations' into data block 'control parameters - variably saturated flow', followed by the number of newton iterations. An example is shown below.
    'control parameters - variably saturated flow'
    ...
    'anticipated number of newton iterations'
    20
    
    Alternatively, user can put this new parameter inside newton iteration settings with the new keyword 'newton iteration settings plus'. An example is shown below.
    'newton iteration settings'
    5.0d-3                        ;increment for numerical differentiation
    20                            ;anticipated number of newton iterations
    60                            ;maxit_vs, max. number of newton iterations
    5.0d-3                        ;tol_vs, convergence tolerance
    0.001                         ;sw_star

12. Add 'ellipsoid fitting ice sheet model' followed by seven parameters describing the model into data block 'initial condition - ice sheet loading/unloading' and 'boundary conditions - ice sheet loading/unloading'. An example is shown below:
    'ellipsoid fitting ice sheet model'
    582882.0                    ! center of ellipsoid, X0, unit is same as input coordinates
    5033160.0                   ! center of ellipsoid, Y0, unit is same as input coordinates
    0.0                         ! center of ellipsoid, Z0, unit is same as input coordinates
    10000.0                     ! principal semiaxes in X axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    2000.0                      ! principal semiaxes in Y axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    1000.0                      ! principal semiaxes in Z axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    60.0                        ! counterclockwise rotation angle (along Z axis) in degree

    For transient ice boundary conditions, add time series of these parameters into prefix.bcice file. An example is shown below:
    ! 2nd column: center of ellipsoid, Y0, unit is same as input coordinates
    ! 3rd column: center of ellipsoid, Z0, unit is same as input coordinates
    ! 4th column: principal semiaxes in X axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    ! 5th column: principal semiaxes in Y axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    ! 6th column: principal semiaxes in Z axis, unit is same as input coordinates
    ! 7th column: counterclockwise rotation angle (along Z axis) in degree
    0.0000E+00 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 1.0000E+03 1.0000E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 1.0000E+03 1.0000E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01
    6.4550E+02 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 1.6031E+04 2.5650E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 1.6031E+04 2.5650E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01
    1.1610E+03 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 2.8833E+04 4.6133E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 2.8833E+04 4.6133E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01
    1.4638E+03 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 3.6355E+04 5.8167E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01 5.9942E+05 5.0464E+06 0.0000E+00 3.6355E+04 5.8167E+03 1.0000E+02 4.5000E+01
    
13. Add 'transform pre-exist surface water to ice' into data block 'control parameters - ice sheet loading/unloading'.

14. Add 'gradient-radius' and set direction character to 'r' (default if not set to 'r'). The direction character line should include the radius centre in x, y, z format. An example is shown below:
    'boundary type'
    'Gradient-radius' 0.0d0
    'r' 0.0  0.0  0.0
    4000.0d0
    5.0d-4

15. Add keyword 'extent of zone: boundary (internal or all) nodes (or cells) within distance to point' followed by point coordinates line and distance range line. An example is shown below:
    'extent of zone: all nodes within distance to point'
    100.0   0.0   200.0    ;coordinates of specified point
    50.0    500.0          ;distance range minimum, maximum

16. Add keyword 'extent of zone: boundary (internal or all) nodes (or cells) within distance to line' followed by points coordinates line and distance range line. An example is shown below:
    'extent of zone: all nodes within distance to line'
    100.0   0.0   200.0    1000.0 0.0 200.0    ;end point coordinates of the specified line  
    50.0    500.0 .true.  .false.              ;distance range minimum, maximum, flags to use sphere extension for end points

17. Add 'read spatial data using master processor' in to data block 'global control parameters'. Without this keyword, the data is read by all the processors.

18. Add 'memory limitation per processor' followed by memory size and unit. An example is shown below.
    'memory limitation per processor'
    1000.0 MB  !or 1.0 GB or 1.0d-3 TB or 1.0d-6 PB.     

19. No keyword is required to use this feature.
20. Add keyword 'minimum aqueous concentration for root respiration' followed by value of minimum total aqueous concentration of each component into data block 'plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake'.
21. No keyword is required to use this feature.
22. Add keyword 'include diffusive flux for third/mixed boundary condition' into section of boundary condition setting. This parameters is zone dependent.
23. Add keyword 'limited output of transient data' into data block 'output control'.
24. No keyword is required to use this feature.

25. The related keywords of component-mineral recycle should be added into data block Data Block 17: plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake. There are two types of parameters: global parameters and zone dependent parameters.

The following keyword are for global parameters.

To define recyclable component-mineral pair, add keyword 'recycle components and minerals' followed by list of component-mineral pairs. An example is shown below.

'recycle components and minerals'
4
'ca+2' 'CaOxalate_ox'   !ca+2 is recycled as CaOxalate_ox
'ca+2' 'calcite'        !ca+2 is recycled as calcite
'al+3' 'kaolinite'      !al+3 is recycled as kaolinite
'ca+2' 'kaolinite'      !no recycle happens as kaolinite has no ca+2

To define return time, add keyword 'recycle and return times'. An example is shown below.

!Define mineral recycle and return times:
!time1: start time of solute uptake for recycle as mineral in current cycle
!time2: start time of mineral return in current cycle
!time3: end time of mineral return in current cycle
!solute uptake before time1 or after time3 are not considered in the recycle.
!Between time1 and time2 (stage1), only solute uptake is considered. 
!Between time2 and time3 (stage2), the total solute uptake in stage1 and 
!solute uptake in stage2 are returned as mineral 
!
!format of 'recycle and return times'
!ntimes: number of cycles
!time1, time2, time3
!time1, time2, time3
!...
!time1, time2, time3

'recycle and return times'
6
0  0.9  1
1  1.9  2
2  2.9  3
3  3.9  4
4  4.9  5
5  5.9  6

When overlap is allowed in mineral return zones, the same return zone can accept mineral return from solute uptake/respiration from different regions. To enable this feature, add keyword 'allow overlap in return zones'. 

'allow overlap in return zones'

By default, solute uptake during mineral return time is also considered as returned mass. To exclude solute uptake in leaf-mineral recycling during return time, add keyword 'exclude uptake during return time'.

'exclude uptake during return time'


The following keyword are for zone dependent parameters.

To define the amount of component recycled as specific mineral, add keyword 'return component to mineral coefficients' followed by list of coefficients.

'return component to mineral coefficients'
0.9    !'ca+2' 'CaOxalate_ox', 90% ca+2 is recycled as CaOxalate_ox
0.1    !'ca+2' 'calcite'       10% ca+2 is recycled as calcite
1.0    !'al+3' 'kaolinite'     100% al+3 is recycled as kaolinite
0.1    !'ca+2' 'kaolinite'     no recycle happens as kaolinite has no ca+2


To define return zones, add keyword 'return zones: box' or 'return zones: cylinder' followed by list of specified zones. An example is shown below.

!format 1: mineral return in box zone
!xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax
!xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax
!...
!xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax 

!format 2: mineral return in cylinder zone
!xcenter, ycenter, zmin, zmax, radius_min, radius_max
!xcenter, ycenter, zmin, zmax, radius_min, radius_max
!...
!xcenter, ycenter, zmin, zmax, radius_min, radius_max

!'return zones: box'
!0.0  1.0  0.0  1.0  0.0 1.0
!0.0  3.0  0.0  2.0  0.0 1.0
!0.0  5.0  0.0  3.0  0.0 1.0


'return zones: cylinder'
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
0  2.5  0  1  0  1
...

26. Add keyword 'exclude diffusive flux for third/mixed boundary condition' or 'exclude diffusive flux for mixed boundary condition' into each zone in data block 'boundary conditions - reactive transport'. 

27. No extra keyword is required.

28. Add keyword 'user specified loading factor' followed by specified loading factor data block 'physical parameters - porous medium'. An example is shown below.

'user specified loading factor'
0.95d0

29. Add keyword 'gradient average spatial weighting' into data block 'spatial discretization' followed by spatial weighting method. An example is shown below.
    'gradient average spatial weighting'
    'centered'    !default
    !'harmonic'
    !'minimum'

30. Add 'pure evaporation' into data block 'global control parameters' when 'root uptake' is not activated.  Detail documentation for root uptake is to be added.
31. Add 'legacy root uptake method' into data block 'plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake'.

32. Add 'root upscale factor - archisimple' followed by three factors into data block 'plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake'. An example is shown below:
    'root upscale factor - archisimple'
    8.0 1.0 8.0        ;x, y, z upscale factor

    or

    'root upscale factor - AS'
    8.0 1.0 8.0        ;x, y, z upscale factor

33. No extra keyword is required.
34. No extra keyword is required.

35. Add 'components decay half time' followed by half time in time unit second or add 'components decay rate constant' followed by rate constant s^-1 into data block . T_half = 0.693/rate_constant. An example is shown below.

    'components'
    1                             ;number of components (nc-1)
    'hto'

    'components decay half time'
    387892800         ;12.3 years

***Bugs Fixed***
1. Fix bug in 'zero flow velocity' when specific storage coefficient is zero. All the matrix entries and rhs are zero. No solver is required for flow.
2. Fix bug in boundary condition output in gen file. The header 'volume   boundary type     hydraulic head' should be 'volume   boundary type     pressure head' for non-density-dependent flow and 'volume   boundary type     pressure' for density-dependent flow.
3. Fix bug in 'output of transient data for interface fluxes' in MPI parallel version. Only the local owned interface fluxes are considered. The limitation is, if the interface pair ivol-jvol belongs to different domain, it is not considered.
4. Fix bug in 'zero flow velocity'. No saturation update should be called when reading initial condition from file.

5. Fix bug in global variable num_cells_per_layer for MPI parallel version. This variable is not broadcasted to all processors. It only affects layered mesh with ice loading/unloading when cells are selected by layer id.
6. Fix bug in porosity output in the gsv file. The calculation of porosity output is only correct if all solids are considered in the mineral input including those "inert" minerals. Now all the porosity output uses pornew variables, which is calculated based on the user specified porosity value in the input file and mineral precipitation/dissolution if update porosity/permeability is turned on. 
7. Fix bug in running ice sheet model for 2D unstructured version. Quadrilateral mesh is not considered in the previous version.
8. Fix bug in density dependent flow. Parameter tds_old and densold_pitzer are not set to the new value during Picard iteration. Only the variable densold = density is there.
9. Fix bug in dfluxvg function in jacrt. The gpivol_ivol, gpivol_jvol should be used instead of gpivol_ivol, gpivol_ivol pair. This affects convergence rate of gas advection cases.
10. Fix bug in reading legacy vtk format. Please note, for legacy vtk format, mixed cell types are not supported.
11. Fix bug in mineral mass balance output format. When number of parallel reaction pathways are considered, the tecplot header is not consistent with columns of data output.
12. Fix bug in binary output for transient data (.gb* files). Some of the unnecessary written is activated in the previous version.
13. Fix bug in harmonic mean for reactive transport problem using unstructured grid method.
14. Fix bug in binary format for transient output when MPI parallel version is used as binary format does not allow rewind operation.
15. Fix bug in surface mesh related output (e.g., icesheet) for MPI parallel version. 
16. Fix bug in stencil width setting for MPI parallel version. The old version skip defaul solver parameter setting when root.dat file exists, causing zero stencil width problem which cannot be supported in domain decomposition.  

17. Fix bug in MPI parallel version realted to the noncollective memory monitor.
18. Fix bug in 'limited output of spatial data'. Logical Unit of 6 (screen output) is turned off when 'limited output of spatial data' is used, resulting screen output to fort.6. 
19. Fix bug in gradient reconstruction for component concentration. This part affects reactive transport with anisotropic material properties. It also reduces numerical diffusion when multipoint flux approximation is used.
20. Fix bug in mass output through specified boundaries for MPI parallel version. The array jatmsb is non-collective.
21. Fix bug in diffusion coefficient calculation in MCD code. The variables mdiff_ic_cvol and mdiff_ix_cvol should not be accumulated overtime. This bug does not affect steady state flow since the function is called only once. But for unsteady state flow, the function is called at each timestep and the diffusion coefficient after first timestep is not accurate.
22. Fix bug in passing increment to gradient reconstruction for aqueous concentration.
23. Fix bug in bubble model for MPI version. Gas_pair setting is put inside output block which is not correct.

24. Fix bug in high order gradient reconstruction for MPI parallel version. The memory monitor inside building control volume interface is not collective. 
25. Fix bug in ary-dry aqueous pressure parameter. The index of this parameter that has been used in root related part is not correctly set in the code.
26. Fix bug in NaN (not a number) in charge balance error output for multicomponent diffusion. 

27. Fix bug in 'read soil hydraulic function parameters from file'. The previous version does not open this feature for general flow problem, but only for root uptake related code. 

28. Fix bug in transient output. 'call updtsvap' and 'call totconc' is commented in local revision 817 whihc is not correct. 
-->
 

***Updates***
1. Set all pi value to 3.141592653589793d0. The previous version use 3.141592653589790d0 in some functions.
2. Replace tab with space in the source code for alignment.
3. Disable water saturation update when initial condition is read from external file and flow velocity is fixed to zero.
4. Update the tiny time parameters from 1.0d-20 or 1.0d-5 in different modules to 1.0d-12.
5. Add courant number calculation to unstructured grid code and enable courant number calculation only when courant target number is less than 1.0d30. 
6. Increase the maximum number of components that can be used in a mineral, secondary specie, gas and etc from 10 to 100.
7. Update output of pore stress. Previous output pore stress in prefix.gsmech files are actually pore stress change over in that timestep.
8. Update domain decomposition option for hexahedral mesh. Interpolate option is turned off for hexahedral mesh to have better decomposition. 

9. Update in 'reading material properties/initial condition ... from vtk file' when structured grid is used. These features are initially implemented for unstructured grid and it is now also available in structured grid version.
10. Update default setting for corss diffusion term by including cross diffusion term for flow problem, including corss diffusion term in advection and excluding it in diffsion term for heat and reactive transport problem.
11. Update the sign in por_stress (porosity change due to mechanical loading stress). Use positive value when mechanical loading is released and negative value when mechanical loading is added. No effect in simulation results except that the output of por_stress is reversed.
12. Update the default gradient reconstruction method of unstructured grid code from 'least square' to 'least square second order' for better convergence rate.

12. Update parameters of 'root respiration and exudation of aqueous phase' and 'minimum aqueous concentration for root respiration' to zone dependent parameters.
13. Remove output of porosity change due to mechanical loading. Porosity value is already availabe and the change can be calculated based on the porosity value.
14. Update 'read initial aqueous concentration from vtk file' or 'read initial aqueous concentration from hdf5 file'. When a component does not exist in the file, assign a small value 1.0d-20 to the component.  
15. Update in reading data from external vtk file. The old version calls rewind before reading a variable which slows down the performance. 
16. Update in tpfrtlc. The update in total aqueous concentration and secondary components cause small difference when transient output is used. The update is disable inside transient output except for batch reactions. 
17. Update in respiration output (*.resp). Replace the last half columns with accumulative total respiration over the elapsed time.
18. Update keyword 'calculate loading efficiency coefficient' to 'calculate loading factor'.

19. Important update on keyword 'root water uptake'. Water pressure optimal value is added under 'root water uptake'.

    Old:
    'root water uptake'
    160.0                      ;water pressure wilting point ! to further reduc the uptake
    1.0                        ;water pressure field capacity
    0.0                        ;root density (optional when density field is specified)

    New:
    'root water uptake'
    160.0                      ;water pressure wilting point ! to further reduc the uptake
    1.0                        ;water pressure field capacity
    10.0                       ;water pressure optimal value (used in Feddes and VG)
    0.0                        ;root density (optional when density field is specified)

20. Important update on keyword 'soil hydraulic function parameters'. A 6th optional parameter 'air dried aqueous pressure head' is required to turn on evaporation function. If not provided, this parameter is zero thus no evaporation is used.

    'soil hydraulic function parameters'
    0.15                        ;residual saturation
    1.20                        ;van genuchten - alpha
    1.38                        ;van genuchten - n
    0.5                         ;expn
    0.0                         ;air entry pressure
    1600.0                      ;psidry (air-dry pressure head)

21. Update format of 'read anisotropic correction angles from file', angles are provided in (alpha,beta,gamma) format.

22. Remove keyword for porosity and tortuosity correction for MCD diffusion, the keyword does not take effect.
23. Update information in the transient output file (*.gb*) with coordinates (x,y,z).

24. Add support for latest PETSc-3.16.
-->



***Benchmark Updates***

1. stripf_usg: use 'two-point flux approximation', or reduce ratio of horizontal dispersivity and transverse dispersivity. The previous version has bug in gradient reconstruction.
2. d45_salt_dome_problem_d133_b3_usg: use 'least square second order' and 'multi-point flux approximation' method, change maxit_sia to 6 and iter_target to 3, and Courant to 1.0 for better convergence rate. 
3. Other unstructured grid examples: use 'least square second order' as default gradient reconstruction method.
4. Delete demonstration examples 'aniso-diff-disp-cond-tensor-V2.0.26.783', replace with 'anisotropy-diff-mcd-V2.1' where analytical solution for anisotropic diffusion is available.

Revision 726
***New Features***
1. Add global to natural ordering by using label. This feature supports reading spatial data from external files for MPI parallel version.
2. Add support to read cell based hydraulic conductivity/permeability from external file for MPI parallel version using unstructured grid. 
3. Add support to scale mesh coordinates for spatial output. This is optional as user can use postprocessing software to do mesh scaling. This feature is only available for unstructured grid version.
4. Add support to read initial condition from hdf5 file. This feature is available in unstructured grid, MPI parallel version where binary format data output is used. The number of processors used to read external file can be different from the number of processors used to generate those external files.
5. Add support of domain information to the xmf file for spatial output.
6. Add support of PETSc V3.11.x.
7. Add support for 'trace' type boundary condition. For flow problem, 'tracer' type boundary condition marks the flow through the control volume. It does not change affect the flow in the domain. For reactive tranpsort, 'tracer' type boundary condition is used as a 'third' type boundary condition. The total mass transported is calculated based on the specified tracer concentration and the flux through the control volume.
8. Add support for steady state flow running in transient mode. For some steady state flow, because of transient effect, the simulation may encounter convergence problem if using steady state flow directly. In this case, transient flow can be used until flow reaches steady state condition.
9. Add support for output of mass through specified boundary, including water (*_i_b.mvc), aqueous phase (*_ic_i_b.mac) and gas phase (*_ig_i_b.mgc). Here i is the index of specified boundary, ic is the index of aqueous component and ig is the index of gas. The format of these three files are similar to *_o.mvc, *_ic.mac, *_ig.mgc except that the storage terms and other source sink terms are not included. Please look at gen file and fls file for detail.
10. Add support for alias activity update. Previous code allow to use 'no update' instead of 'no_update' and 'double update' instead of 'double_update'. The new code add support '_',' ',and '-' as delimiters. Now the code supports the following activity update alias: 'no_update', 'no update', 'no-update'; 'double_update', 'double update', 'double-update'; 'time_lagged', 'time lagged', 'time-lagged'. 
11. Add support for automatic cell-node re-ordering if the ordering is not counterclockwise for 2D plane in 3D space. MIN3P-HPC requires cell-node ordering in counterclockwise that obeys RHT rules.
12. Add support for 'update root length density from file'.

***Usage***
1. No extra keyword is required.
2. No extra keyword is required.
3. Add the following keyword and parameter to data block "spatial discretization".
    'scale factor for mesh output'
    1.0 1.0 10.0             ;scale factor for x, y and z axis.
4. This feature require external file prefix.domain.h5 and prefix.ivs.h5. These two files can be renamed from prefix_domain.h5 and prefix_*.gsp.h5. Add the following keyword to data block 'initial condition - variably saturated flow'.
    'read initial condition from hdf5 file'
5. No extra keyword is required.
6. No extra keyword is required.
7. Add the following keyword to the block 'boundary conditions - variably saturated flow' 
    'boundary type'
    'tracer' rdummy                   ;tracer type
  and the following keyword to the block 'boundary conditions - reactive transport'.
    'boundary type'
    'tracer'
8. To use this feature, please set flow to transient flow and add the following keywords to data block 'control parameters - variably saturated flow'.
    'run steady state flow in transient mode'

   To determine whether the flow has reached steady state, user can specify the tolerance for steady state by using relative difference value and absolute difference value divided by time step. Please add the following keyword and parameters after keyword 'run steady state flow in transient mode'. By default, these tolerance is preset to 1.0d-6 for both relative difference and absolute difference.

   'tolerance for steady state'
   1.0d-6    ! relative difference divided by time step (day) for hydraulic head between current time step and previous time step
   1.0d-6    ! absolute difference divided by time step (day) for hydraulic head between current time step and previous time step

   User can also control if the heat transport has reached steady state by adding following keyword to data block 'control parameters - energy balance'.
   'tolerance for steady state'
   1.0d-6    ! relative difference divided by time step (day) for temperature between current time step and previous time step
   1.0d-6    ! absolute difference divided by time step (day) for temperature between current time step and previous time step

9. Add the keyword 'output of mass through specified boundary' followed by number of specified boundary and boundary zone definition into 'Data Block 8: output control'. An example is shown below

   'output of mass through specified boundary'

   'end of zone'                              ;end of current zone

   'number and name of zone'                  ;keyword for zone
   2                                          ;number of specified boundary

   'extent of zone: boundary nodes only'      ;keyword for zone selection (other keywords are available)
   0.1 0.1  0.0 0.0  0.15 0.20                ;coordinates for zone selection
   'end of zone'                              ;end of current zone

   'update root length density from file'

Please note that data sharing of coordinates is used in the rld file to save memory in the file. An example of the data format is shown below:
   title = "dataset transp-updrld"
   variables = "x", "y", "z", "rld"
   zone t = "Initial root length density" i =   30, j =   30, k =    1,  f=point

***Bug Fixed****
1. Fix bug in storage term calculation (storvs.F90) for flow problem and retrieving physical parameters for scaling (rtrvpprm.F90). The variable por in storvs and rtrvpprm is a global scalar that it saves the porosity of the last zone only, causing the inconsistence when these functions are called. This bug affect all the problems with heterogeneous porosity distribution. 
2. Fix bug in direct solver configuration (SuperLU, MUMPS) for PETSc release version 3.9+. 
3. Fix bug in mass balance error output for root water uptake (e.g., .mve file).
4. Fix bug in mass balance output when 'append results' is used (e.g., .mac file). Some code is missing in the previous version. 
5. Fix bug in periodic time step control. Small time step range (e.g., time range < global maximum time step) may be skipped 
6. Fix bug in reading enthalpy change (dhc) for secondary aqueous sepcies, should use 'dhcx(ix) = -dhc' instead of 'dhcx(ix) = dhc'.
7. Fix bug in allocating iamdisoa array. Size of iamdisoa should be (naq*2+1).
8. Fix bug in 'reassign rates based on combined mineralogical parameters'. This part was ported from MIN3P isotope model but may cause significant convergence problem for other cases. This section is kind of hardwired and it is now modified. This section is used only if isotope pair is set, e.g., nip > 0.
9. Fix possible bug in setting tortuosity, porosity, saturation et al. in infcrt_g and infcrt_mcd. These variables should be added as each of the same iisav is looped more than once. For example, given mesh with 17x1x25 nodes, for such a 2D problem, ix=15, iy=1, iz=13, idim=1, ipair=1 ==> iisav = 1050 and ix=15, iy=1, iz=14, idim=1, ipair=2 ==> iisav = 1050. The old code set gsatij(iisav) = satav and gsatij(isymm(iisav)) = satav which always uses the second node pair while the new code uses the average value. Need further check here.
10. Fix bug in velocity output for DGM model. The variable dgflux_cd_dgm and neflux is not initialized and should be set to zero when there is no gas phase.
11. Fix bug in OpenMP parallel version when alias of activity update is used. The previous OpenMP code can only work with activity update of 'no_update', 'double_update' and 'time_lagged'. Now it works with 'no_update', 'no update', 'no-update', 'double_update', 'double update', 'double-update', 'time_lagged', 'time lagged' and 'time-lagged'.
12. Fix bug in 'mixed' boundary type condition when flow is out of domain. Should use free advective mass outflux.
13. Fix bug in updating reactive transport boundary conditions. It was called one time step earlier than it should be. The method to check time is also updated accordingly. Now both flow and reactive transport can update boundary conditions at the same time.
14. Fix bug in binary porosity output for unstructured grid version using MPI. The porosity output is not consistent with ascii output. This bug is local.
15. Fix bug in using porosity for retrieving physical parameters for scaling. Porosity is not passed correctly when porosity is read from external file. This bug is local.
16. Fix bug in transient output for unstructured grid code using MPI parallel version. The outupt is duplicated in each processor.

***Updates***
1. Update 'reverse cell-node ordering' for prism and hexahedral cell. The cell node should obey right hand thumb rule and the outward normal of the bottom face (first three nodes) should point to the cell center.
2. Add support of tab delimiter in vtk file.
3. Change the output format (e.g., e12.4, e16.8) to 1pe15.6e3 to avoid converting the data 1.23456E-123 to 1.23456-123 when written to the file. For fortran, it is the default feature to write very small value in the format 1.23456-123. This is mainly caused by the variables without initialization. 
4. Update cell-node ordering of 2D coordinates in 3D. The cell-node ordering should be obey RHT (right hand thumb) rule. For example, for coordinate in XZ plane, the node-cell is in counterclockwise where the outward normal points to the Y direction. This update affect all xz coordinates since the previous code does not obey RHT rule for XZ plane.
5. Separated source/sink term from oxidation/reduction reactions and intra-aqueous kinetic reactions. The previous version output the two terms together as intra-aqueous reactions. The output file of mac is changed and is not backward compatible with previous version.



Revision 690
***New Features***
1. Add feature to resume output from breaking point when simulation is restarted, with support to dynamic check if the file exists and meet the data appending requirement. There are two restart files generated during MIN3P simulation, with breaking point t1 and t2. When simulation is restarted, user can choose one restart file to restart simulation. The results can be appended in two different methods: the normal results appending and the legacy results appending. The former one appends results immediately after the breaking point wheile the latter one appends results after it reaches the second breaking point. For example, assuming t1 < t2, if results are appended in normal mode and t1 is chosen as restart time, the code removes the old transient output data after t1 and appends the new results after t1; if results are appended in legacy mode, user can only choose the earlier restart file (t1) and the code keeps the old transient output before t2 and appends the new results after t2. When simulation is restarted, there is loss of significance that may cause blips in the transient output at the first time step (e.g., storage change over time). In this case, user can use legacy mode to avoid such a problem.

2. Add feature to run simulation based on structured grid using unstructured grid method and generate results of spatial output in vtk format. This feature works for 2D and 3D and does not support 1D.

3. Add feature to force the buffer data back to external file when restart point (time to create restart file) is reached. Writing to disk is slow so that the code actually write the data to a buffer in memory until the buffer is filled or the file is closed (default setting by most compilers). When simulation does not exit normally, the output data in the buffer is lost. In this circumstance, appending the transient data is impossible when simulation is restarted. In the updated version, every time when restart point is reached, the buffer is flushed to make sure all the output data is saved back to the external file in time.

4. Add periodic maximum time step control. This feature support to use different maximum time steps in the specified time ranges.

***Usage***
1. For the normal results appending, add the keyword 'append results' into data block 'global control parameters'. This keyword should be used together with keyword 'restart'.  For the legacy mode results appending, please use the earlier restart file as restart condition and add the keyword 'append results in legacy mode' into data block 'global control parameters'. The input keywords are shown below.

  'restart'
  !'append results'                      ;Append results after the first restart time
  'append results in legacy mode'        ;Append results after the second restart time

  'backup frequency'
  100


2. Add keywords 'use unstructured grid method' followed by 'structured spatial discretization' into data block 'spatial discretization', as shown below.

  'spatial discretization'
  'use unstructured grid method'
  'structured spatial discretization'
  1                             ;number of discretization intervals in x
  21                            ;number of control volumes in x
  0. 1.0                        ;xmin,xmax
  1                             ;number of discretization intervals in y
  1                             ;number of control volumes in y
  0. 1.0                        ;ymin,ymax
  1                             ;number of discretization intervals in z
  41                            ;number of control volumes in z
  0. 2.00                       ;zmin,zmax

3. Default feature, no keyword is required.

4. Add keyword 'periodic maximum time step' into data block 'time step control - global system', followed by a list of time ranges and maximum time steps. An example is shown below

  'periodic maximum time step'
  3                             ;number of time step list
  0.1  0.2   0.001              ;time start, time end, maximum time step
  0.5  0.6   0.0001             ;time start, time end, maximum time step
  0.8  1.0   0.01               ;time start, time end, maximum time step


***Updates***
1. Use timestep (delt_t0/r10) saved in the restart file as initial timestep when simulation is restarted. In the previous versions, when restart file is used, it starts with the minimum timestep to run the simulation.


***Bug Fixes***
1. Fix bug in gbm header format. Vairable "charge bal [%]" is in a separated new line in the previous versions.

***Notes***
1. When 'append results' or 'append results in legacy mode' is used, user will find the following information in the prefix_o.fls file, indicating the results afterward are appended results to the previous simulations. The simulation can be restarted consecutively.

  ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++
  output of appended results
  ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

or

  ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++
  output of appended results in legacy mode
  ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

2. If the results files are generated by the old code, the transient output data may be not completed (last record earlier than the last breaking point). In this case, the last record is before restarting time, an warning information is given in prefix_o.fls file, as shown below. 

  warning - last data record in file prefix_x.abc is before restarted time

3. When 'append results' is used but the results of transient output in the previous simulation do not exist, the outputs of initial condition (time = 0.0) in the transient results are skipped. Other than that, the results output are the same as normal 'restart'.  At the restart point, the time steps between output is ignored. Compared to the previous code, this will cause some difference in transient output if 'number of skipped output times' is larger than 1, due to extra output at restart point.

4. The accumulative mass balance and accumulative relative mass balance error are recalculated based on the breaking point (restarted time) saved in the transient output file. If the last record is before breaking point, last record is used instead of breaking point record and there will be numerical error in the acculative relative error.

5. There may be a bug in gas bubble model (case 506-4) that there are very small difference when different 'number of skipped output times' is used. This problem occurrs in all MIN3P versions with gas bubble model. Since the new code changes transient output frequency (e.g., force output at restart point), it causes some difference in the results because of the potential bug in gas bubble model. If 'number of skipped output times' is 1 (no skip), there is no difference between old code and new code. The difference is very small and can be ignored. Further check is needed to debug this problem in gas bubble model.

Revision: 675
***Summary***
Port ArchiSimple code (root water uptake and evaporation) to MIN3P; add support for node velocity reconstruction using Perot’s method; update maximum number of columns and variables in the result file from 50 to 1000; update 'twothird-mix' surface area update; fix bug in gas tortuosity correction for default option 'same as aqueous' in revision 633; fix bug in cell center dual volume method for unstructured grid version.

***New Features***
1. Merge ArchiSimple code (root water uptake and evaporation) to MIN3P. Thanks to Celine and Fred for providing ArchiSimple code and initial code porting to MIN3P.
2. Add support for node velocity reconstruction using Perot's method.

***Usage of New Features***
1. Usage of ArchiSimple code is to be added, an example of ArchiSimple block is shown below.

  ! Data Block 1: global control parameters
  'global control parameters'
  'transpiration 2D couplage rld'

  .true.                                                   ;varsat_flow
  .false.                                                  ;steady_flow
  .false.                                                  ;fully_saturated
  .false.                                                  ;reactive_transport
  .true.                                                   ;plant_uptake

  !Data Block 17: plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake
  ! ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  !
  'plant transpiration and passive/rejective uptake'
  ! ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  !NB :If none of these options is activated, the root length densities
  !NB considered are those specified in the 'root water uptake' section. 
  !NB In the 'update-internal' option, ivol refers to the control volume ID. 

  !'read root length density from file'     ; 1D
  !'update - internal'		              ; 1D model: 
  !rld(ivol)=exp(a+b*sqrt(z(ivol))+c*time+d*sqrt(z(ivol))*time)
  !-12.48 1.725 0.0017 0.0003961	  
  !; a, b, c, d parameters of the model for 'update - internal'

  'done'

2. Add 'use perot velocity reconstruction' into Data Block 3: spatial discretization.

***Code Update***
1. Update the maximum number of columns and variables in the result file from 50 to 1000.  In the previous version, the maximum number of columns (variables) is 50 while the maximum number of variables ranges from 50 to 1000, which is inconsistent.  When number of output columns exceeds 50, it will be continued in the next line.  There is no problem in loading the data into tecplot.  However, for those who use external software, this may cause trouble in loading data, e.g., the gsd files in benchmarks_new_add\sit-clogging-V1.0.598\clogging.

2. Update 'twothird-mix' surface area update method.This feature updates reactive surface area using twothirds power relationship considering reaction direction and porosity change.  For the primary mineral (those initially present with a volume fraction > phinucthrd), it uses twothird power relation for dissolution, but initial area for precipitation.

Formula of dissolution reaction: 
Area = Area_init*(phim(im)/phim_init(im))^(r2/r3)

Formula of precipitation reaction: 
Area = Area_init

For secondary mineral (those initially present with a volume fraction <= phinucthrd), it uses twothird power relation for dissolution, but initial area for precipitation.

Formula of dissolution reaction: 
Area = Area_init*(phim(im))^(r2/r3)

Formula of precipitation reaction: 
Area = Area_init

When porosity update is used, an additional term (pornew/porinit)^(r2/r3) is used in multipling surface area.

3. Update velocity reconstruction for variably saturated flow velocity output using Perot’s method (Blair Perot, 2000, Conservation properties of unstructured staggered mesh schemes, Journal of Computational Physics, 159, 58-89).  It was observed that this method does not always preduce good reconstruction, e.g., for stript_usg case, the reconstruction velocity is not as good as previous version which is based on the gradient reconstruction of the node.  Also, the advective and diffusive velocity in reactive transport need further check.

4. Update of random generator in ArchiSimple code.  By default, ArchiSimple code use time dependent random generator.  This will make the result not identical.  User can now use constant seed in random generator to keep the result identical for the same input file.  

***Benchmarks Update***
1. benchmarks_new_add\root-uptake-ArchiSimple-V1.0.704

***Documentations Update***
1.	MIN3P_THCm_UserManual
2.	MIN3P_THCm_USG_UserManual

***Bugs Fix***
1. Fix bug in default gas tortuosity correction in Revison V633.  When gas tortuosity correction is not specified, the default option is 'same as aqueous'.  But in Revision V633, this default option is missing.
2. Fix bug in cell center dual control volume method.  The control volume interface area is not accurate when the control volume interface does not cross the edge at the middle point of the edge. This cause small changes in \benchmarks_usg\react\d43_density_dep_heat_solute_usg_cc.

***Summary of code verification (test running)***
Comparison of the results for all examples obtained by the current and the previous MIN3P-THCm version (V633) shows no difference except the aforementioned benchmarks:
benchmarks_new_add\sit-clogging-V1.0.598\clogging
\benchmarks_usg\react\d43_density_dep_heat_solute_usg_cc
The difference is caused by code updates and bugs fix.



Revision: 633
***Summary***
Merge branch https://biot.eos.ubc.ca/svn/min3p_thcm/branches/dsu_new_add_18sept to Trunk. The new features includes but not limit to: 1) new boundary conditions (third-evap, mixed-evap, free-drainage) types; 2) exponential decay function for kinetic reaction; 3) activity coefficients output; 4) multi-point flux approximation (MPFA) and other optimization for unstructured code.

***New Features***
1. Add support of activity coefficients output.  New files .gsac, .gbac and .lbac are created when activity coefficient output is activated.

2. Add support for tortuosity correction using 'millington-quirk experimental' or 'fix diffusion' based on the following formula.
            case('millington-quirk experimental')
              diffcoff = diff_p * (sat_p**b_mq) * (por**(a_mq+r1))               
            case('fix diffusion')
              diffcoff = diff_p  

3. Add support to reverse cell-node ordering for unstructured mesh.  For MIN3P unstructured grid version, counterclockwise cell-node ordering is required.  In case the cell-node ordering is in clockwise ordering, this keyword can be used to maker internal conversion for cell-node ordering.

4. Add support to read initial condition and other parameters from vtk file.  By default, the unstructured grid version uses tecplot data format when reading these variables from the external file.  User now can use 'read ... from vtk file' to activate this feature.  The file name of external data is changed accordingly by adding suffix '.vtk'.

5. Add support for 'minutes' time unit.

6. Add support for 'multi-point upstream weighting' for unstructured grid code.  In structured grid code, upstream is based on two-point flux approximation.  For unstructured grid, upstream can be based on multi-point flux aproximation that the upstream point can be defined by the gradient direction, not just by the hydraulic head.  This features provide better convergence for highly nonlinear problem.  When ‘upstream’ weighting is used, 'multi-point upstream weighting' is used as a default option in simulation using unstructured mesh. 

7. Add keywords 'multi-point flux approximation ...' and 'two-point flux approximation ...' to include or exclude cross-diffusion term.  These keywords have the same effect as 'include cross diffusion term ...' and 'exclude cross diffusion term ...'

8. Add keyword 'use control volume half face center'.  This keyword has the same effect as 'use separated interface centers'. 

9. Add keyword 'threshold of failed timestep ratio'.  This keyword is used to terminate simulation when the ratio of failed timesteps over total timesteps exceeds the specified threshold.

10. Add support for 'gas tortuosity correction' using 'assigned tau'.  Gas tortuosity value can be read from zone input or from external file.

11. Add exponential decay function for kinetic reaction.

12. Add 'third-evap' boundary condition for transport boundary.  Compared to 'third' type boundary condition, 'third-evap' allows inflow mass flux but no outflow mass flux, which means the mass is retained in the domain, e.g., water evaporation but solute is retained.

13. Add support for 'free-drainage' boundary type for flow. For free drainage, the gradient of total head is equal to 1 and the gradient of the pressure head is equal to 0.

14. Add 'mixed-evap' boundary condition for reactive transport. Different from 'mixed' boundary type, 'mixed-evap' boundary type does not allow mass out when flow flux is negative, like evaporation.

***Usage of New Features***
1. Add 'output activity coefficients' into Data Block 'output control'. 

2. Add the following part into Data Block 'control parameters - reactive transport'
    'tortuosity correction'
    'millington-quirk experimental'                    
    2.0d0                         ; a_mq
    4.2d0                         ; b_mq

    or 

    'tortuosity correction'
    'fix diffusion'

3. Add 'reverse cell-node ordering' into Data Block 'spatial discretization'.

4. Change 'read ... from file' to 'read ... from vtk file' to read data from vtk file format.  For example, when 'read initial mineral volume fractions from file' is used, the system will read in prefix.min as the external data file.  To use vtk file format, user need to use 'read initial mineral volume fractions from vtk file' and change the external data file name to prefix.min.vtk. 

5. Change the time unit in Data Block 4: 'time step control - global system' to 'minutes'.  An example is shown below.
    'time step control - global system'
    'minutes'                     ;time unit
    0.0                              ;time at start of solution
    60.0                            ;final solution time
    1.0                              ;maximum time step
    1.0E-10                       ;minimum time step

6. Add 'multi-point upstream weighting' into Data Block 6: 'control parameters - variably saturated flow'.  Note, this feature is only available when 'upstream weighting' is used in the flow system.  It's not avaiable in 'centered weighting'. This is a default option for unstructured grid code.  To use standard upstream weighting, please add the keywords to 'two-point upstream weighting' into Data Block 6.

7. Add 'multi-point flux approximation', 'multi-point flux approximation for flow', 'two-point flux approximation for flow' ... into data block 'spatial discretization'.

8. Add 'use control volume half face center' into data block 'spatial discretization'.

9. Add 'threshold of failed timestep ratio' followed by threshold value into data block 'time step control - global system'.  As shown below, when more than 50% of timesteps fail, the simulation will be terminated. 

    'threshold of failed timestep ratio'
    0.5d0

10. If 'gas tortuosity correction' is set to 'manual', the old prefix.gvs file is used as gas tortuosity. 
    If 'gas tortuosity correction' is set to 'assigned tau', user needs to specify gas tortuosity in the block 'physical parameters - porous medium', example input is

    ! ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    'number and name of zone'
    1
    'background1'
    0.25                         ;porosity

    0.50                         ;tortuosity for gas phase

    'end of zone'

    If 'gas tortuosity correction' is set to 'assigned tau' and external file (prefix.torgas) is used as gas tortuosity, user needs to add keyword 'read gas tortuosity field from file' in the block 'physical parameters - porous medium', example input is

    'physical parameters - porous medium'
    1                             ;number of property zones

    'read tortuosity field from file'
    'read gas tortuosity field from file'

11. Modify redox database to add 'exponential T^a' term.  Example is shown below.

    'exponential T^a'  2
    'biomass1'     0.5
    'biomass1'     0.3

12. Change the boundary type to 'third-evap'.
 
    'number and name of zone'
    1
    'inflow boundary'

    'boundary type'
    'third-evap'

    'concentration input'
    1.0         'free'           ;co3-2
    2.0         'free'           ;h+1

    'extent of zone'
    0.0 1.0  0.0 1.0  100.00 100.00

    'end of zone'

13. Add the following keywords to the block of 'boundary conditions - variably saturated flow'.  Note: the parameter following 'free-drainage' is a dummy parameter.  The data 0.0 is ignored after reading.  This is used only to meet the requirement of input data format.

    'boundary type'
    'free-drainage'  0.0     ;flux

14. Use 'mixed-evap' instead of 'mixed' in Data Block 'boundary conditions - reactive transport'.

***Code Update***
1. Add support for PETSc V3.10 version. 

2. Set default unit to atm for keyword 'partial gas pressure'.  To use Pascal as input gas unit, please use keyword 'partial gas pressure (pa)'.  The following keywords are equal.
    'partial gas pressure'
    0.3947694E-03                ;atm

    'partial gas pressure (pa)'
    40.0                         ;Pa

2. Update column alignment for species and units in fls file.

3. Update the code to be compatible with latest Intel Compiler XE 2019 when OpenMP is used.  The problem has been reported to Intel (Request 03681401) as there is bug in Intel Compiler that does not parse the OpenMP code correctly when unstructured grid capability is used.

4. Update of output information when default solver configuration is used.

5. Replace '\' with '/' for 'parallel solver configuration file' path as '\' only works for Windows.

6. Disable '.masmcd' and '.mssmcd' file creating for MCD case as the output for '.masmcd' and '.mssmcd' is not implemented in mbal_mcd.F90. 

7. Length of btypezn is changed from 12 to 16 to account for the length of 'free-drainage'

***Benchmarks Update***
1.	Benchmarks_new_add/ SOM_V1.0.632

***Documents Update***

2.	MIN3P_THCm_ValidationReport
3.	MIN3P_THCm_Parallel_UserManual
4.	MIN3P_THCm_USG_UserManual

***Bug Fixes***
1. Fix bug in screen output of dbs_dir check. 

2. Fix bug in velocity output for unstructured grid under unsaturated condition.  Spatial weighting (e.g., upstream) is missing in calculate velocity.  This bug affects velocity output file for unstructured grid version only.

3. Fix bug in totviscnew calculation.  This variable is global, however, in the previous version, totviscnew is changed when loop over control volumes and connections in jacrt and then passed directly to calculate mass balance, causing error in calculating mass balance.  The difference in mcd, mac, mae may be not significant. 

4. Fix bug in mineral volume fraction assignment when read from file.  In the previous code, the volume fraction read from file is compared to the value phic(im) from the zone and assign phic(im) instead if the value is below phic(im).  In the updated code, the volume fraction read from file is compared to phimin(im) instead.  The bug fix will affect the results in sulfur-V1.0.431.

***Verification***
Comparison of the results for all examples obtained by the current (Benchmarks_V1.0.632) and the previous (Benchmarks_V1.0.609) MIN3P-THCm version shows the differences in Diff_Benchmarks_V1.0.632_vs_V1.0.609. These differences are caused by the code updates or bug fixes. The results are manually checked through BeyondCompare and tecplot postprocessing. The differences are comparatively small and generally unrecognizable in the visualization.

Revision: 608
Author: dsu
Date: Tuesday, September 11, 2018 11:26:48 AM
Message:
***New Features***
1. Add support for reading initial condition from vtk file for flow, density dependent flow and heat transport. File name is previs.ivs.vtk.
2. Add support for separated controls for cross diffusion term in flow, heat and reactive transport.
3. Add control for velocity output averaging for flow and density dependent flow problem. By default, the velocity of control volume ivol is averaged based on the velocity of control volume interfaces linked to ivol. For the nodes at the boundary of different zones with highly different material properties (e.g., conductivity), the velocity distribution of control volume interfaces may has a very wide range, making the velocity interpolation not accurate. In this case, a simplified method, based directly on the gradient of this node and minimum conductivity of this control volume is used to calculate the velocity. 

***Usage***
1. Add 'read initial condition from vtk file' in block 'initial condition - variably saturated flow' and/or 'initial condition - energy balance'. 
2. To include or exclude cross diffusion term in flow, heat and reactive transport, add the following keywords in block 'spatial discretization'.
3. Add the following command to block 'spatial discretization'. If the coefficient of variation of velocity distribution is bigger than this value, then use simplified method, otherwise, use normal velocity interpolation method.

'variation coefficient for velocity average'
0.5d0

***Updates***
1. Update the maximum line length in the input file from 72 to 256. Please note the maximum keywords length is still 72 while the maximum data length, e.g., database path, can be up to 256. 
2. Update keyword to exclude cross diffusion term for flow and reactive transport to 'exclude cross diffusion term for flow', 'exclude cross diffusion term for heat transport', and 'exclude cross diffusion term for reactive transport'

***Bug Fixes***
1. Fix bug in timestep modification when update boundary condition is used. The previous code "if (itsrc.le.ntsrc.and.tsrc(itsrc)-tiny_time.lt.time) then" in timeloop.F90 is buggy that when tsrc is much bigger than tiny_time, it will fail as tsrc(itsrc)-tiny_time is equal to time, e.g., tsrc(itsrc)=4380000.00 (12000 years), tiny_time = 1.0d-10, time=4380000.00 (12000 years). tsrc(itsrc)-tiny_time = time. To make it stricter, avoid minus a very small value from a very big one due to the precision limit in computecode.
2. Fix bug in velocity output when cross diffusion is not used.
3. Fix bug in cross diffusion calculation for isotropic tensors.
4. Fix bug in DGM model gas flux calculation when Maxwell Stefan module is used. The previous code and MIN3P-DGM-2012 code pass the wrong variable to function ms_fluxdg_s. This will affect mgc files.
5. Fix bug in velocity output in density dependent flow. The velocity here is pore velocity, not darcy velocity.
6. Fix bug in nbrt_usg initialization in initbcrt.F90. nbrt_usg=0 should be placed outside zone loop.

Revision: 606
Author: dsu
Date: Monday, August 20, 2018 6:32:34 PM
Message:
New Features, Bug Fixes and Code Updates in MIN3P-THCm-USG

***New Features***
1. Check if the material properties have been set to the volume or duplicated set. Give error information if the material properties are missing or error information if not set.
2. Add support for PETSc3.8 and PETSc3.9 version.
3. Add newton iteration divergence check. If the maximum update value in newton iteration has four consecutive increasing, skip and reduce timestep.
4. Add 'gradient', 'gradient-top', 'gradient-bottom' initial and boundary condition type setting. 'gradient-top' means use the top surface coordinates instead of current selected node coordinate. 'gradient-bottom' means use the bottom coordinates instead of current selected node coordinate.
5. Add keyword to read skempton coefficients from zone selection commands.
6. Add support fot Newton divergence check for the code.
7. Add water freezing/thawing process to general flow and reactive transport problem. For the previous version, it is only available when heat transport is considered.
8. Add water freezing relative conductivity fitting curve. 

***Usages***
5. Add following command line and data in Block 'physical parameters - variably saturated flow'
  'specific skempton coefficient'
  1.0
6. Add 'enable divergence check' in control parameters - for flow and reactive transport.
7. Add the following commands in 'control parameters - variably saturated flow' to activate water freezing/thawing module for flow problem.
  'water freezing temperature'
  -1.0
  'water freezing conductivity'
  1.0d-30
  Add the following command in 'control parameters - reactive transport' to set reaction rate to zero or very small value when water is freezing.
  'water freezing reaction rate'
  1.0d-30	
8. Add the following keywords and parameters to block 'control parameters - variably saturated flow'. Make sure keyword 'water freezing conductivity' is removed, otherwise, it will use fixed 'water freezing conductivity' first. The output of temperature - fitted relative conductivity is exported in the gen file.
  'water freezing relative conductivity curve'
  0.01273  22.47  2.002  1.002  ;parameter a, b, c and d for relative conductivity curve relcond=d+(a-d)/(1.0+((temperatureC+2.0)/c)^b)
  -1.0     1.0    0.01   1.0    ;minimum temperature, maximum temperature, minimum relcond, maximum relcond

***Bug fixes***
1 Add convergence check for fsflow and ddfsflow, Fix bug in ddfsflow, fsflow when newton iteration is not converged.
2 Set to zero flux by changing bcondvs(ibvs) = r1 to bcondvs(ibvs) = r0 in seepfdd.F90 and seepface.F90.  
3 Fix bug in valovapour, use ups_heat instead of ups_flow for diff_energybal.
4 Fix bug in timeloop for picard iteration, comment out "not_converged_sia=.false.", otherwise, picard iteration may return without convergence check.
5 Fix bug in updateing delt_tds value when timestep needs to be reduced. The previous code in timeloop.F90 does not include this part when timestep is reduced. If this value is not reduced accordingly, the convergence is not improved when timestep is reduced, which may cause iteration failure.
6 Fix bugs in storage term calculation in density dependent flow. Fix bug in pressure difference calculation when 'average density in z' is used. Bug is benign and does not show significant difference. 
7. Fix bug in using uninitialized variable in jacddfs_energybal, causing extreme values.
8. Fix bug in gasdecay mass balance calculation. 
9. Fix bug in OpenMP related race condition.
10. Fix bug in PETSc KSP solver when initial solution meet the convergence results (iteration=0). Previous code will treat this as non-converged situation.
11. Fix bug in passing solver parameter from solver.cfg (For PETSc and LIS solver type) back to MIN3P code. Some convergence checking need these parameters to be set. By default, these parameters are from input file. However, if solver type is speicied to PETSc or LIS, these parameters should be updated accordingly. 
12. Fix bug in 'read hydraulic conductivity field from file'. If this command is used, van Genuchten soil parameters and/or specific storage coefficient are ignored, causing it works only for saturated flow in the previous version.
13. Fix bug in hydraulic conductivity output when hydraulic conductivity or permeability is read from file. The previous code will only output when hydraulic conductivity or permeability is read from zone setting.
14. Fix bug in PETSc residual output for user-defined convergence. 
15. Fix bug in unit conversion for surface-complex species output from "mol/L H2O" to "mol/L Bulk", saturation*porosity should be applied for this unit conversion. The previous code lack porosity term.
16. Fix bug in hydraulic conductivity output in gsv file. The previous code zone pointer always point to the last zone. This will cause difference in sit-clogging-V1.0.598\clogging\benC1a benchmark.
17. Fix bug in total velocity output in gsa file. The total velocity term is not averaged while other (adv, dif, mig) terms are averaged, making tot != adv + dif + mig.

***Code updates***
1. Update convergence information output of picard iteration.The previous picard convergence output is not for the current timestep, but for the previous timestep.
2. Update in output format for real*8, use 1.0E-123 format instead of 1.0-123, which cannot be recongnized by paraview. 
3. Modify makefile for PETSc version preprocessing definition. User does not need to specify PETSc version. The current active PETSc version ($PETSC_DIR) will be used.
4. Remove initial guess of solution vector norm check for parallel version. This part is not required and the time spent in norm calculation does not worth.
5. Update support for sytem enviornment variable OMP_NUM_THREADS. By default, if number of threads is specified in the solver configuration file, the code will use this value, otherwise, it will use sytem enviornment variable OMP_NUM_THREADS.
6. Update default lis solver option. The new default lis solver option is " -p ilu -tol 1.0E-20 -maxiter 5000". This new default option works better in MIn3P code than the default LIS solver option where no preconditioner is used.
7. Disable output of hydraulic conductivities (.hycx). The output is actually scaling factor for permeability (frac). 
8. Update activities guess in guess.F90. Previous code use actv(ic) = r10**(-2.8980d0)*totco(ic) when DGM model is ported. Now we switch back to actv(ic) = vhoff(-2.7945d0,1.2647d-3,tempkel,tempks,rgascal) and actv(ic) = actv(ic)*totco(ic) for non-DGM cases. This difference caused by this is minimal. The following cass are affected.
  benchmarks_new_add\edbasin-base-V1.0.473
  benchmarks_new_add\co2seq-water-freezing-V1.0.377
  benchmarks_new_add\dusty-gas-model-V1.0.303\deviss139
  benchmarks_standard\reactran\amd_ex
  benchmarks_standard\reactran\amd_ex2
  benchmarks_standard\reactran\degas
  benchmarks_standard\reactran\perm3_new 

***New Features of USG***
1. Add 'fixed flow velocity' for USG mesh. For USG mesh, it is impossible to generate strictly one-direction flow unless the mesh is fully orthogonal. In this case, the flow velocity can be forced to one direction with specified value. 
2. Add support for dual volume type using 'cell center' method. This method use cell center connection as control volume interface.
3. Add support for node/cell selection coordinates constrain. This can be combined used for node/cell selection by material id, by node/cell id or by polygon/polyhedral selection. If the node or cell center is within this box (xmin xmax ymin ymax zmin zmax), the selected node/cell is valid. It does not work for 'extent of zone: internal/boundary/cell nodes/cells only'.
4. Add support for 'gradient' initial condition type for variably satuarted flow
5. Add support for 'gradient-top' and 'gradient-bottom' initial condition type for variably saturated flow. This is only applicable to unstructured grid with layered mesh. If 'gradient-top' is used, it will use the top surface node above the given node in calculating gradient, if 'gradient-bottom' is used, the bottom node below the given node is used.
6. Add support for cell-based hydraulic conductivity and permeablity. 
7. export mesh to pflotran mesh format (implicit)
8. Add new keyword 'boundary flux direction'. This is same as 'boundary flow direction'. Same for atmospheric boundary, e.g., rain.
9. Add node/cell selection by material properties. Note, user need to add node_matids and cell_matids in the vtk file and only vtk mesh file support reading material properties.
10. Add node/cell selection control for general extent of zone operator. 

***Usage***
1. Add the following keywords and data to block 'control parameters - variably saturated flow'
   'fixed flow velocity'
   0.0d0 0.0d0 0.0d0             ;fixed flow velocity in (x,y,z) direction in m/day, correction for USG
2. use 'cell center' after keyword 'control volume method'.
3. Put the following commands in the zone selection
   'extent of zone: coordinates constraint'
   xmin xmax ymin ymax zmin zmax
4. If dir_grad == 'x', then pos_grad=xg(ivol), else if dir_grad == 'y', then pos_grad=yg(ivol), else if dir_grad == 'z', then pos_grad=zg(ivol). hinitial = h0_grad + slope_grad*pos_grad
   'initial condition'
   'gradient' dummy            !dummy value is ignored
   'dir_grad'
   h0_grad
   slope_grad
   'extent of zone'
   400000.0 600000.0  4000000.0 6000000.0  -2000.0 1000.0
5. If dir_grad == 'x', then pos_grad=xg(ivol-top), else if dir_grad == 'y', then pos_grad=yg(ivol-top), else if dir_grad == 'z', then pos_grad=zg(ivol-top). hinitial = h0_grad + slope_grad*pos_grad
   'initial condition'
   'gradient-top' dummy            !dummy value is ignored
   'dir_grad'
   h0_grad
   slope_grad
   'extent of zone'
   400000.0 600000.0  4000000.0 6000000.0  -2000.0 1000.0
6. Same as node based file reading. Add keywords 'cell based hydraulic conductivity' and/or 'cell based dispersivity for reactive transport'.
7. Add keyword 'export mesh to pflotran mesh format' in spatial discretization block.
8. Add keyword 'boundary flux direction' when setting the second boundary type for flow/evaporation boundary.
9. Node/cell selection by material properties is similar to node/cell selection by id. The keywords are shown below.
   'extent of zone: boundary nodes by material id'
   or
   'extent of zone: internal nodes by material id'
   or
   'extent of zone: all nodes by material id'
   or
   'extent of zone: boundary cells by material id'
   or
   'extent of zone: internal cells by material id'
   or
   'extent of zone: all cells by material id'
   !Followed by 
   'read data'
   or
   'read data from file'
10. Add the following keywords before or after 'extent of zone' 
   'extent of zone: boundary nodes only'
   or
   'extent of zone: internal nodes only'
   or
   'extent of zone: all nodes'
   or
   'extent of zone: boundary cells only'
   or
   'extent of zone: internal cells only'
   or
   'extent of zone: all cells'


***Notes***
1. MIN3P-THCm-USG work with PETSc-3.8+ dev version and/or PETSc PETSc-3.9+ release version.
2. HDF5 write in Linux system take large memory space due to the unreleased memory of hdf5 write buffer. When HDF5 is used (USG, Binary), it takes three times more memory than the code uses ascii output or disabled output. This is a system problem, not the HDF5 library, as in Linux, if the kernel needs more memory, it grabs as much as there is physical memory. See detail in https://support.hdfgroup.org/HDF5/faq/linux-mem.html.
